Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the bio structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 311. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 651 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 311 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the bio structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 651. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The bio variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 617 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 816 and intersection form of rank 2.
